# Mutants for tools/mutate.sh: one deliberate bug each, for the rules that guard against losing the user's work.
# Block format (see docs/engineering.md, "Mutation tool"):
#   === REQ-NNNN-xx short name of the bug
#   file: path of the source file, from the project root
#   test: the meson test names (space separated) that must fail, for example core-resize or ui-0005
#   --- find
#   the exact text to find (must occur once; may span lines)
#   --- replace
#   the text that takes its place
# The text of a mutant has no blank line (a blank line ends the block). A mutant is KILLED when one of its tests fails,
# and SURVIVES when they all pass.

# ---- resize and its removed pixels, the undo history (0002, 0004) ----

=== REQ-0004-22 undo of a resize does not put the removed pixels back
file: src/core/history.c
test: core-resize
--- find
              img->px[(size_t) y * (size_t) s->old_w + (size_t) x] = s->removed[i++];
--- replace
              i++;

=== REQ-0004-22 redo of a resize forgets the fill color
file: src/core/history.c
test: core-resize
--- find
        return easel_image_resize (img, s->new_w, s->new_h, s->fill) == EASEL_RESIZE_OK;
--- replace
        return easel_image_resize (img, s->new_w, s->new_h, 0) == EASEL_RESIZE_OK;

=== REQ-0004-23 a resize step costs 12 bytes per removed pixel instead of 4
file: src/core/history.c
test: core-resize
--- find
    return s->n_removed * sizeof (uint32_t) + BYTES_PER_STEP;
--- replace
    return s->n_removed * 12 + BYTES_PER_STEP;

=== REQ-0004-24 a resize to the same size makes an undo step
file: src/core/history.c
test: core-resize
--- find
    return EASEL_RESIZE_UNCHANGED;
--- replace
    (void) 0;

=== REQ-0004-20 a shrink keeps the wrong corner of the picture
file: src/core/image.c
test: core-resize
--- find
          memcpy (row, img->px + (size_t) y * (size_t) img->width, (size_t) kept_w * sizeof (uint32_t));
--- replace
          memcpy (row, img->px + (size_t) y * (size_t) img->width + (img->width - kept_w), (size_t) kept_w * sizeof (uint32_t));

=== REQ-0004-26 a resize that fails still records an undo step
file: src/core/history.c
test: core-resize
--- find
  if (r != EASEL_RESIZE_OK)
--- replace
  if (0)

=== REQ-0002-17 a lone step bigger than the history budget is dropped
file: src/core/history.c
test: core-resize
--- find
  while (h->bytes > h->budget && h->len - h->first > 1)
--- replace
  while (h->bytes > h->budget && h->len - h->first > 0)

  g_signal_emit (self, signals[SIGNAL_PENDING_CHANGED], 0);
  g_signal_emit (self, signals[SIGNAL_SELECTION_CHANGED], 0);
  gtk_widget_queue_draw (GTK_WIDGET (self));
  return TRUE;
